[[AC PARTY APPLAUSE]] The outbreak in China, which soon influenced the world and our country, has spread to more than 70 million people, and no one has heard the name of Covid-19 disease caused by the new type of coronavirus, and has not been affected by it. In view of the course of the disease, some countries, including Turkey, seem to have partially controlled the epidemic. From the 2019-2020 school year, the pandemic, which has been in emergency action plans, has become our reality. From the purpose of education to their shapes and environments, all things have been reviewed, while the foreseeable changes have been required to be implemented. The Ministry of National Education is suddenly planning and taking steps to establish both the age and the future predictions on a stable basis, with great sacrifice and effort towards the pandemic process in front of us. I am pleased to point out that a similar achievement in health in Turkey's fight against the epidemic took place in the field of education. We have found that we are more prepared for remote education than most countries in the world, especially Europe. The EBA system, run by the Ministry of National Education, is progressing quite smoothly, given the number of students continuing to teach. The Education Information Network (EBA) serves about 8 million of our students during the outbreak of Covid-19, which has been renovated in line with developing technology and in line with 2023 educational vision targets. Again, I can safely say that private schools have passed a successful test in the process. In the pandemic process, the efforts and efforts of the Ministry of National Education have demonstrated the accuracy of our educators' predictions. When we put aside the challenges and obligations that the pandemic process puts in front of us, we need to analyze the 2002 and 2021 figures to know where and how our Ministry of National Education came from today. The overall budget for the Ministry of National Education was 956 thousand dollars in 2002, while in 2021, 211 billion 993 million dollars was 15.7 per cent; in 2002, 10.1 per cent was earmarked from the general budget. The amount of cash and the same assistance the National Ministry of Education made directly to students was $8 billion, 96 million in 2003, and that was $30.1 billion in the budget of 2021. At 526,470 in 2002, we have 974.837 teachers today. Our number of teachers appointed during our administrations corresponds to 71 per cent of the current number of formal teachers. Our number of courses was 343.194 in the 2002-2003 school year, and 595.124 today. The number of students per school year in 2002-03 is 36 in primary school, 30 in secondary school, and 24 in elementary school and 19 in secondary school today. This painting shows that our government is walking with confidence to grow quality human resources in the field of education. It's our sole goal to run to the troubled level of civilization by growing visionary generations that have written the consciousness of trust and responsibility in your mind and heart. Thanks to this visionary perspective, the Ministry of National Education has determined the theme of the 2019-2020 year of education and education as a useful model, brand year and design, to help develop the country's economy and technology by patenting technical information produced in vocational and technical education schools.
